## Scientific And Domain Assessments

### Exact taxon and source lineage

identity: supported. Targets: NCBITaxon:2511821.

NCBITaxon:2511821 denotes Archaeoglobaceae archaeon at SPECIES rank beneath NCBITaxon:2511820, genetic code 11. All 9 carried ancestors agree with pinned NCBI and current EFetch in identifiers, literal labels and ranks. This is the exact placeholder taxon, not an inferred culture or a higher-rank family/order.

### Name classes and nomenclatural scope

nomenclature: supported. Targets: NCBITaxon:2511821.

The literal NCBI designation Archaeoglobaceae archaeon is preserved. Pinned and current NCBI supply no synonyms. No nomenclatural validity, registered deposit, type material or measured phenotype is inferred from the designation.

### Imported subtree and source assertion units

completeness: supported. Targets: NCBITaxon:2511821.

Complete direct reads of twelve pinned tables, including ignored files, verify one NCBI NAME assertion and zero imported strains; the pinned parent graph has no descendants. GTDB carries 31 qualified broad mappings, with primary cluster count 31 and 30 further clusters. The attestation count is the primary cluster size, not the sum of all source clusters or only target-labelled members. No LPSN or culture/strain inventory match is present.

### Qualified species mapping and target scope

grounding: supported. Targets: NCBITaxon:2511821.

All nineteen pinned GTDB shards were searched using exact terminal species tokens, preserving internal genus suffix underscores. All 31 cluster counts agree with native mappings (135 total selected source members). 23 clusters have only NCBITaxon:2511821 in both primary taxids/species-taxids and the complete reverse mapping inventory, contradicting the fixed other-NCBI-taxa clause. Other clusters contain genuinely mixed taxids. Pooling multiple GTDB species onto the NCBI placeholder still supports broadMatch; native NCBI lineage remains distinct from GTDB classification.

### Unchanged native status and history

provenance: supported. Targets: NCBITaxon:2511821.

EXACT grounding and SEEDED mapping agree with exact NCBI identity. The sole seed event at 2026-09-13T17:54:04Z agrees with the latest raw/ATB/StrainInfo extraction time. Native status and history remain unchanged.

## Findings

### pooled-note-other-taxa: 23 pooled-mapping notes incorrectly assert other NCBI taxa

minor / open / confirmed; issue key: gtdb-broadmatch-note-other-taxa-2511821.

All nineteen pinned GTDB shards were searched using exact terminal species tokens, preserving internal genus suffix underscores. All 31 cluster counts agree with native mappings (135 total selected source members). 23 clusters have only NCBITaxon:2511821 in both primary taxids/species-taxids and the complete reverse mapping inventory, contradicting the fixed other-NCBI-taxa clause. Other clusters contain genuinely mixed taxids. Pooling multiple GTDB species onto the NCBI placeholder still supports broadMatch; native NCBI lineage remains distinct from GTDB classification. - Retain all 31 mappings, primary count31 and 30 further-cluster attestation; preserve actual mixed-taxid membership and native NCBI lineage.

- Assessment bounded to captured source versions and exact target/query terms. Source-name wording and isolate designations do not establish culture deposits, phenotypes or mechanisms.
- Reused actual native validation on the unchanged shared base 9b5a8fff5185b38dc5c77bb35235152c81ea7765; the recorded cwd and timestamps identify the original execution. These native corpus/source inputs are unchanged; only new review files differ. This receipt is not a new run on the batch branch and does not validate subsequently saved review bundles.
- No generated record or history was edited; curation actions remain proposed.
- Fresh-base canonical recapture preserved all previously reviewed input hashes; original captures and actual transition receipts remain retained.
- Every new bundle still requires its own validate/save/check recipe; baseline native receipts do not claim these later steps have run.

    or culture/strain inventory match is present.

- assessment_id: identity
  area: identity
  topic: Exact taxon and source lineage
  outcome: supported
  summary: NCBITaxon:2511821 denotes Archaeoglobaceae archaeon at SPECIES rank beneath
    NCBITaxon:2511820, genetic code 11. All 9 carried ancestors agree with pinned
    NCBI and current EFetch in identifiers, literal labels and ranks. This is the
    exact placeholder taxon, not an inferred culture or a higher-rank family/order.
  target_ids:
  - NCBITaxon:2511821
  evidence_ids:

  - taxonomy-inventory
  - ncbi-live
  - scope-row
  - path-row
- assessment_id: nomenclature
  area: nomenclature
  topic: Name classes and nomenclatural scope
  outcome: supported
  summary: The literal NCBI designation Archaeoglobaceae archaeon is preserved. Pinned
    and current NCBI supply no synonyms. No nomenclatural validity, registered deposit,
    type material or measured phenotype is inferred from the designation.
  target_ids:
  - NCBITaxon:2511821
  evidence_ids:
  - record
  - taxonomy-inventory
  - ncbi-live
  - lpsn-inventory
  - mapping-policy
- assessment_id: completeness
  area: completeness
  topic: Imported subtree and source assertion units
  outcome: supported
  summary: Complete direct reads of twelve pinned tables, including ignored files,
    verify one NCBI NAME assertion and zero imported strains; the pinned parent graph
    has no descendants. GTDB carries 31 qualified broad mappings, with primary cluster
    count 31 and 30 further clusters. The attestation count is the primary cluster
    size, not the sum of all source clusters or only target-labelled members. No LPSN
    or culture/strain inventory match is present.
  target_ids:
  - NCBITaxon:2511821
  evidence_ids:
  - record
  - taxonomy-inventory
  - bacdive-inventory
  - culture-inventory
  - gtdb-species
  - gold-organisms
  - mediadive-taxa
  - madin-taxa
  - bactotraits-taxa
  - genome-catalog-1
  - genome-catalog-2
- assessment_id: taxonomy-mapping
  area: grounding
  topic: Qualified species mapping and target scope
  outcome: supported
  summary: All nineteen pinned GTDB shards were searched using exact terminal species
    tokens, preserving internal genus suffix underscores. All 31 cluster counts agree
    with native mappings (135 total selected source members). 23 clusters have only
    NCBITaxon:2511821 in both primary taxids/species-taxids and the complete reverse
    mapping inventory, contradicting the fixed other-NCBI-taxa clause. Other clusters
    contain genuinely mixed taxids. Pooling multiple GTDB species onto the NCBI placeholder
    still supports broadMatch; native NCBI lineage remains distinct from GTDB classification.
  target_ids:
  - NCBITaxon:2511821
  evidence_ids:
  - record
  - gtdb-mappings
  - gtdb-species
  - mapping-policy
  - equivalence-contract
  - seed-owner
  - genome-catalog-1
  - genome-catalog-2
  - gtdb-full-scan
- assessment_id: native-status
  area: provenance
  topic: Unchanged native status and history
  outcome: supported
